# TC -【Fitness CRM】- [A-01][会員管理（一覧）] (Member Management — List)

**Scope**: The member **list** screen only (route `/members`, PO spec `A-01`, L1), including the two dialogs that live only inside it: the select-all confirmation dialog and the bulk primary-contract change dialog.

**Out of scope** (link-only — a case verifies navigation intent, never the destination): member detail `A-01-01` (`member-detail`), member edit form (`member-form`), blacklist screens, emergency-suspension screen, and the shared header store-scope selector.

Driven by [test-plan.md](../test-plan.md) — Unit 1 (Member list). This is a single-unit feature; every FR-001–FR-019 traces here.

## Source note

Cases are grounded **only** in these sources (implementation code was **not** read):

- **Spec** — `specs/019-member-list/spec.md` (User Stories 1–3, Edge Cases, FR-001–FR-019, Key Entities, Assumptions).
- **Requirement docs** — `.cache/fitness-spec/crm/requirements/A-01.md` (FR-001, FR-002, FR-020, FR-026, 権限マトリクス L563–L583) and `G-06.md` FR-011 (promotion-code filter).
- **UI prototype** — `.cache/fitness-crm-ui/src/pages/member-list.tsx`@`46253e3` + parity checklist `specs/019-member-list/checklists/parity.md` (PAR001–PAR086).
- **Shared checklists** — `tests/checklists/common-function-checklist.md` (incl. the **Real-API Integrated Screen** section), `tests/checklists/common-gui-validate-checklist.md`.
- **Directive** — `specs/019-member-list/tests/test-plan.md` (Unit 1: unit, sources, deltas D1–D13, gaps B1–B8, coverage focus). A directive, never an oracle.
- **Revision 2026-11-19 (ladyroom)** — `[Ladyroom]` cases **TC-125 – TC-137**. Sources: PO specs `.cache/fitness-spec/crm/requirements/E-02.md` (`261109_v6`: FR-016 sale only to matching members; FR-008 only 女性 matches 女性のみ, only 男性 matches 男性のみ, その他 / 回答しない / 未設定 match neither), `G-01.md` FR-M002 (利用できるエリア, 性別制限) and `A-01.md` FR-020 (surrounding flow), all at `fitness-spec@9e0e07e`; internal spec `spec.md` User Story 3 scenario 6 + FR-016a; UI prototype `.cache/fitness-crm-ui/src/pages/member-list.tsx`@`9428dc2` (`bulkContractOptions`, `noBulkContract`, placeholder 「変更できる主契約はありません」, rows carry a hidden `gender`) + `src/data/facility-areas.ts` (`canSellToGender`); API facts only from `src/lib/openapi.real.json` (`PlanListItemDto.genderCondition`, `MemberListItemDto` — no `gender`, `POST` / `GET /api/v1/admin/members/bulk-plan-changes[/{jobId}]`). Directive: test-plan header, §3–§6 ladyroom rows (LR1–LR3, AREA-_), §8, §9 step 8, and the 029 test plan §7e–§9 (fixtures `QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-_`). Implementation code was not read.

All sources were available and readable. No case is `Block` for a missing-source reason.

**Integration round — phase 2 (Round 2).** The screen is fully integrated: it calls the **real dev backend** `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com` (`NEXT_PUBLIC_REAL_API_URL`) with a real staff session; no mock data path remains. Server-owned outcomes (server-side validation, `E-XXX-nnn` codes, 401 expiry, server-enforced scope, real pagination totals, persistence, two-tab conflicts) are therefore **real cases with real expected results**, not deferrals. `api-integration-output/member-list/report.md` is read as the **known-gap register** (see §Known gaps) — never as an expected result.

**TC-120** was added 2026-09-21 (the 店舗 dropdown must offer only the accessible stores). **Cases 2026-07-24 → TC-090** were authored in Round 1 and re-executed 2026-09-17 against the real API. **TC-091 – TC-119 were added 2026-09-18** by this pass to close the phase-2 and 1024px-layout dimensions; their execution columns are empty.

## Test-data reference (shape only — from the prototype `MEMBERS` sample + spec)

> **Phase-2 rule**: the rows below describe the **shape** of a roster row and the vocabulary a case must recognise. They are **not** rows that exist on the dev backend. The backend is **shared, mutable and never reset**, so no case may assert a total, a page count or a specific member it did not create. Every count-sensitive expected result reads "verify at execution".

**Dates are evaluated against the real current date.** The prototype's `TODAY = 2026/04/30` does **not** apply: compute the last-visit / join-period bounds at run time (直近1週間 = today−7d, 3週間以上来館なし = today−21d + never-visited kept, 1ヶ月以上来館なし = today−30d + never-visited kept). The bulk-change effective date is the 1st of the month **after the run date**.

### Data & environment limitations (gate execution — see per-case Notes)

- **Shared backend, no seed reset.** Anything a case creates stays. The list screen itself can only mutate data one way: 一括変更 **books a real plan-change job** that cannot be cancelled from the CRM. Every execute needs the user's explicit go-ahead, must use the smallest possible selection (1 member), and the case must record the member id + target plan + target month so the booking is traceable (see §Known gaps B2).
- **No member can be created from this screen**, so the "create what you destroy" rule degrades to "book the minimum and report it". Never book a change on a member another run is using; never edit or delete pre-existing master data.
- **One role only today.** The real Cognito pool is not in `.env` (it still points at the mock `localhost:9230`), so sessions come from an injected `SESSION` cookie carrying a **Headquarter** token (TTL ~1 h). Cases for System / Manager / Staff / Observer / Trainer are `Block (B1)` until BE supplies per-role tokens.
- **Free text is not user-editable on this screen** — a long name, a hostile string or a hyphenated promotion code can only be tested if the dev data already carries one, or BE seeds it. Those cases run against the **longest value actually present** and are `Block (B5/B6)` when nothing long/odd enough exists.
- **Count-dependent cases** (multi-page selection, ellipsis, single-page footer, out-of-range page) depend on the roster size on the day — verify the totals at execution instead of asserting the ones recorded in an earlier run.

### Ladyroom fixtures and members (2026-11-19, TC-125 – TC-137)

The QA 主契約 come from the [029 plan §8](../../../029-equipment-management/tests/test-plan.md) (created by 023 Unit 2 `[Ladyroom]`, 有効, no holders, linked to the JOYFIT main fixture store). The `genderCondition` column is what `PlanListItemDto.genderCondition` should carry for each. Members are **pre-existing and read-only**: locate them at execution by the 性別 on their member detail (A-01-01 FR-004), preferably in the fixture store, and record their ids in the case Notes. ## Priority definitions

| Priority | Meaning                                                             |
| -------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| High     | Core/main-flow cases, and UI issues immediately visible to the user |
| Medium   | Validation cases                                                    |
| Low      | Rare/edge cases                                                     |

## Common preconditions

> **Credentials (phase 2).** This repo has **no `src/lib/auth/demo-users.ts`** — there is no per-role credentials sheet for this screen. The app's own login is unusable while `NEXT_PUBLIC_COGNITO_ENDPOINT` points at the mock `localhost:9230`, so a session is established by injecting the cookie `SESSION = encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify({ access_token: <real staff token>, token_type: 'Bearer' }))` on `localhost` (token from BE, **TTL ~1 h**). Only a **Headquarter** token exists today ⇒ every other role is `Block (B1)`. The header 「デモ: ユーザー切替」 switcher (needs `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_ENV=development`) changes the **mock** session only and does **not** change the role the real API authorises.

| ID   | Precondition                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| PC-A | Signed in as **Headquarter** (or System); header store scope = 全店舗; `/members` loaded, data finished loading. Selection column, bulk bar, and row 編集 are available.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| PC-B | Signed in as **Staff**, scoped to own store; `/members` loaded. _(no token today → `Block (B1)`)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| PC-C | Signed in as **Observer**, scoped to own store; `/members` loaded. _(no token today → `Block (B1)`)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| PC-D | Signed in as **Manager**, scoped to managed store(s); `/members` loaded. _(no token today → `Block (B1)`)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| PC-E | Signed in as **Trainer**. _(no token today → `Block (B1)`)_                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| PC-F | PC-A satisfied and the 詳細フィルター (detailed filter) panel is open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| PC-G | PC-A satisfied and at least one member row is selected (bulk action bar visible).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| PC-H | Account with **≥2 accessible stores** (so the store filter renders) and a switchable header store scope.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| PC-I | PC-A satisfied in **two** browser tabs/sessions sharing the same account (two-tab conflict cases).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| PC-J | PC-A satisfied, then the session token is **expired or removed** (delete the `SESSION` cookie / wait past the ~1 h TTL) without reloading the page.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| PC-K | PC-A satisfied at viewport **1024×768** (the supported floor). Below 1024px is out of scope.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| PC-L | PC-A satisfied, plus the ladyroom fixtures: the QA 主契約 of the [029 plan §8](../../../029-equipment-management/tests/test-plan.md) — `QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-F` (女性のみ area), `-M` (男性のみ), `-N` (なし), `-NOBODY` (女性のみ + 男性のみ) — exist (created by 023 Unit 2 `[Ladyroom]`), are 有効, have no holders and are linked to the fixture store; the 029 §7e pre-flight (AREA-BE, AREA-MOCK) is done; members F1 … U are located (see §Ladyroom fixtures and members). |

With a Headquarter session, switching the header scope from 全店舗（本部） to 「JOYFIT24 渋谷 (dev)」 narrows the roster to 17 rows and removes the 店舗名 column; adding ステータス=有効 on top gives the banner 「全 169 件中 12 件を抽出中: 有効」 and the 詳細フィルター button badge reads **1**. The header scope is therefore excluded from both the condition summary and the active-filter count, which is what this case asserts. **But the denominator is wrong** — 169 is the global total, not the scoped 17: see BUG-A01-14 on TC-097. Block (B5) — dev has no name that is an unbroken max-length token (the longest value, 100 chars, contains spaces — see TC-081), and this screen cannot create one. Ask BE to seed a 255-character unbroken name to close this case. EXECUTED 2026-09-22 at **1024x768** by injecting the value at the **network layer** (a Playwright route rewrote the `GET /admin/members` response body), not by writing to the shared dev backend — the rendering path is the real one, only the payload was substituted. Row F3-0000049 was given 氏名 = a 50-character unbroken ASCII token plus a 50-character unbroken full-width katakana token (101 chars including the single space), which is the **maximum the system can hold**: the edit form and `PATCH /admin/members/{memberId}` both cap 姓 and 名 at `maxLength: 50` each, so the 255-character default assumed when this case was written is unreachable by design — the case's boundary should be read as 50 per part. RESULT: the layout holds — no page-level horizontal scroll (scrollWidth 1024 = clientWidth), no element wider than the viewport, no overlapping controls, the 氏名 column keeps its 176px width, the row height stays 48px, and 会員ID / ステータス / 主契約 / 入会日 / 最終来館日 plus the action cell all stay visible and reachable; the value truncates with an ellipsis inside its 160px cell (scrollWidth 1168 vs clientWidth 160). OBSERVATION (the same one as TC-081, counted once): the truncated name exposes **no tooltip/title**, so the full value cannot be read from the list — the prototype specifies no tooltip for this cell, so this is PO's call. Nothing was created (the request failed). **What is actually wrong is documentation**, and it belongs to BE: swagger's `PromotionCodeListItemDto.code` example is `JF24-SPRING-7K2M` (hyphenated), the create schema says only 「must match the scope naming rule」 without stating the rule, and the campaign screen's own 「キャンペーンコード」 rule is 「店舗ID＋英数字5桁」 (e.g. `STR01-A1B2C`) — three sources implying hyphens are legal while the implementation demands uppercase alphanumeric. Minor, but it half-reintroduces the 'failure looks like no match' confusion — worth aligning the banner with the error state. Standing BE item (unchanged): `POST /admin/promotion-codes` refuses a hyphenated code with 'code must be uppercase alphanumeric', while swagger's example `JF24-SPRING-7K2M`, the unstated 'scope naming rule' and the campaign-code rule 「店舗ID＋英数字5桁」 all imply hyphens are legal — a documentation fix on BE's side. |

RUN 2026-09-18 (real Cognito login now works; real dev API, 166-member roster). **BE-owned finding — re-verified 2026-09-21.** The server DOES scope the default query and then lets an explicit parameter override it. With the Staff session's own token: `GET /admin/members?page=1&limit=25` returns `totalItems: 5` (Headquarter gets 169 on the same call), but `GET /admin/members?storeId=00000000-…-0201` returns `totalItems: 17` and `…-0202` returns `6` — byte-for-byte the same counts and the same member numbers (JF-9000024, JF-9000019, JF-9000018 / F3-9000020, F3-9000015, F3-9000011) that Headquarter gets. So a Staff token reads any store's roster simply by naming it. FR-019 expects Staff to see only their own store. (`GET /auth/crm/me` returns `storeId: 00000000-…-0203` for both roles; the earlier note's `stores: []` was a probe artefact — the response carries `storeId`, not `stores` — and is withdrawn, it is NOT the explanation for this behaviour.) The screen-level half (opening /members?store_id=<other store> as Staff) could not be reached: the header scope makes the screen send storeId=store-003 and the list request 400s (known gap B3), so the screen showed 会員一覧の取得に失敗しました. Trainer, by contrast, IS refused server-side: 403 `E-AUTH-100`. **BE-owned. Re-confirmed 2026-09-21** on all three non-HQ roles, now that their sessions reach the screen properly: each account's own scope is `00000000-…-0203` (5 members), yet a hand-made request with `storeId=00000000-…-0201` returns **17** members to the Staff, Manager and Observer tokens alike — the same roster Headquarter gets. The default query is scoped correctly (Staff/Observer 5, Manager 0 without a storeId), so the server knows the caller's scope and simply does not validate the `storeId` it is handed. FR-019 expects Staff to see only their own store. At screen level the list never asks for another store (TC-080), so this is reachable only by editing the request — which is exactly what this case prescribes. Trainer is refused correctly (403 `E-AUTH-100`). **Fixed 2026-09-29 (BUG-A01-12 closed).** The server now validates the `storeId` it is handed: with `storeId=00000000-…-0201` (and `…-0202`, `…-0204`, or a random uuid) the Staff, Manager and Observer tokens all get `400 E-VAL-001` `details[{field: "storeId", code: "not_found", message: "storeId is outside the caller store scope"}]`; their own store `…-0203` still returns 200 (Staff/Observer 5, Manager 0 — no 管轄店舗), and Trainer stays 403 `E-AUTH-100`. The refusal is a 400 rather than a 403, which is what the live Swagger now documents for `storeId` ("Narrows WITHIN the caller's store scope, never widens it … → 400 E-VAL-001", API-093 §3.2). Screen level as Staff: `/members?store_id=…-0201` sends only `storeId=…-0203` (header scope wins) and renders the 5 JOYFIT 横浜 (dev) members with no toast — no out-of-scope member is ever rendered.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

Under the 全店舗 scope and for role-scoped accounts the banner is correct (Staff: 「全 5 件中 3 件を抽出中: 有効」). When a Headquarter session pins the header scope to one store (「JOYFIT24 渋谷 (dev)」, 17 members) and applies 有効, the banner reads 「全 169 件中 12 件を抽出中: 有効」 — the scoped total is not what is shown. **Not a screen defect**: the number is `pagination.totalAllItems` exactly as the API returns it (`?storeId=…-0201&includeTotalAll=true` → totalItems 17, **totalAllItems 169**), and the screen cannot derive a scoped baseline without an extra request, which this project does not do. Recorded `Block` (API-owned value) pending a BE/PO decision, NOT `NG`. **For that decision**: swagger defines `totalAllItems` as 「every OPTIONAL query parameter reset to its default, with required parameters and your role/store scope still applied: the number you get back by pressing 条件をクリア」 — and on this screen 条件をクリア does not clear the header store scope (TC-031), so by that definition the value should be 17. What is undecided is whether a `storeId` sent by the header scope counts as 'scope' (kept) or as an optional filter (cleared). Re-run once BE answers. EXECUTED 2026-09-22, both halves. (1) Graceful degradation — with `pagination.totalAllItems` forced to **null** (injected into the response by a Playwright route, the state the server reaches when it declines the count under load, API-093 TBD-A01-LIST-COUNT-CAP) and ステータス=有効 active, the banner drops the 「全 N 件中」 prefix entirely and reads 「172 件を抽出中: 有効」 — it never falls back to 「全 172 件中 172 件」, so it never claims nothing was filtered out. (2) Normal path — under the 全店舗 scope and for role-scoped accounts the denominator is right (Staff: 「全 5 件中 3 件を抽出中: 有効」). OPEN QUESTION, not a screen defect (user's decision 2026-09-21, so no bug id): when a Headquarter session pins the header scope to one store the denominator is the global roster (「全 230 件中 …」 rather than the scoped count) because the server returns `totalAllItems` ignoring `storeId`. Swagger defines the field as 「every OPTIONAL query parameter reset to its default, with required parameters and your role/store scope still applied: the number you get back by pressing 条件をクリア」, and 条件をクリア does not clear the header scope — so BE/PO must settle whether a header-sent `storeId` counts as scope (kept) or as an optional filter (cleared). **Re-run this case once that is answered.** Re-run as soon as a staff session exists. RUN 2026-09-18 (real Cognito login now works; real dev API, 166-member roster). Filters and sort survive, the page number does not. Entering /members?status_group=active&sort_by=joinedAt&sort_order=desc&page=3 (6 pages exist) renders page 1 — 「1-25 を表示」 — and the URL is rewritten without `page`; a hard reload of a page-3 URL does the same. The status filter and sort ARE restored (banner 「全 165 件中 140 件を抽出中: 有効」, trigger 有効, rows sorted by 入会日 desc) and the search term is restored too. Browser Back reaches …&page=3 and correctly renders 「51-75 を表示」, so the screen can honour the parameter — it is dropped only on a fresh load/reload. RE-RUN 2026-09-21 after the fix — **BUG-A01-11 closed**. `/members?status_group=active&sort_by=joinedAt&sort_order=desc&page=3` now renders 「全 142 件 / 6 ページ / 51-75 を表示」 and keeps `page=3` in the URL; a hard reload of that URL reproduces the same page, rows and order, with the search term, filters and sort all restored. The out-of-range guard still works: `?page=999` settles on `?page=7` showing 「151-169 を表示」 (TC-053 unaffected). A role with a single accessible store never sees this filter, and that hiding is asserted by TC-088 — not here. Split out of TC-089 on 2026-09-21 after the dropdown was observed to be fed by the unfiltered store master (`GET /admin/stores`), which only looks correct because it was exercised with Headquarter. **known gap B3** — the accessible-store set still comes from the mock user context, so for Manager/Staff/Observer the header scope pins a mock store id, the 店舗 filter is not rendered and the roster itself 400s; the dev Manager account also resolves to a single store. Expect `Block (B3)` until the header scope reads the real store master. ~~Read together with BUG-A01-12: the server does not reject an out-of-scope `storeId`, so the option list is the only thing standing between a role and another store's roster.~~ (BUG-A01-12 fixed 2026-09-29: the server now refuses an out-of-scope `storeId` with 400, so a wrong option would fail rather than leak.) Block (B3) — verified 2026-09-21. With a Headquarter session the 店舗 dropdown is filled by GET /admin/stores?limit=20&sort=name&order=asc&page=1 (the plain master) and lists all 8 stores incl. the closed FIT365 仙台 (dev / 閉店); that is correct for HQ. With the Staff session the 店舗 filter is not rendered at all and no /admin/stores request is made, so the subset behaviour could not be observed. The Manager account on dev resolves to a single store and its roster 400s on the mock header scope id (store-003), so no role with a 2+ store subset exists to test against today. RE-RUN 2026-09-21 — B3 is no longer the blocker, but the case is still unexecutable: **no dev account has 2+ accessible stores**. The Manager account resolves to the single store `00000000-…-0203` (same as Staff/Observer), so the 店舗 filter is not rendered for it at all and there is nothing to compare against the master. Headquarter is offered all 9 entries of the real store master (全店舗（本部） + 8 stores, each with its code and brand), which is correct for that role. Ask BE to assign a second 管轄店舗 to the Manager account to close this case. RE-RUN 2026-09-21 — investigated with the user's authorisation to edit the Manager account, and **it cannot be unblocked that way**. The staff edit form offers only 「所属パターン A: 店舗直接紐づき（1店舗）」 or 「パターン B: FC企業紐づき（1社 → 管轄全店舗）」 — the two are mutually exclusive and neither grants two arbitrary stores. Pattern B would work, but of the 8 dev stores only **one** (JOYFIT24 渋谷 (dev)) has an `fcCompanyId` at all (`…-0601`); the other seven have none, so any FC company still resolves to a single store. Nothing was changed. To close this case BE must attach a second store to an FC company (or create an FC company owning ≥2 stores) and put the Manager account on pattern B.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## Execution summary

**Full phase-2 run 2026-09-18** by Claude (black-box / Playwright) against the app on `http://localhost:3000` and the **real dev backend** `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, roster **164 → 166 members** (it is shared and changed during the run), default page size 25. Viewports: **1024×768** for every layout/long-value case and **1440×900** for the wide check.

- **Sessions are real now.** Earlier today `.env` still pointed at the local cognito-local mock (`:9230`) which was not running, so nothing could be executed. After it was switched to the real dev Cognito pool, all **six** role accounts (`lodu4289+1 / +headquater / +manager / +staff / +trainer / +observer@gmail.com`) signed in through the app's own login, and the permission block was executed for real for the first time.
- **Data written to the shared backend** (authorised by the user, kept to the agreed minimum): three plan-change bookings for **2026-10-01** — `F3-9000003` → Fit365 Family (dev), `F3-9000004` → Fit365 Family (dev), `F3-9000004` → Joyfit24 Kids (dev). Two members touched in total. They **cannot be cancelled from the CRM**; ask BE to clear them. Every other bulk attempt was a refusal and wrote nothing. No member, master record or account was edited or deleted.
- **Three previously-open bugs are fixed and closed**: `BUG-A01-08` (氏名/ステータス sorting), `BUG-A01-09` (double-submit on 実行), `BUG-A01-10` (footer on a single-page result). Three new bugs were found; the two FE ones (`BUG-A01-11`, `BUG-A01-13`) were **fixed and re-verified on 2026-09-21**, leaving the BE-owned `BUG-A01-12` open.

**Reset 2026-10-29 (price basis):** the API now stores plan / option / 休会費 amounts tax-included (BE MR !880, API-085 v0.12 / API-087 v0.14) and the screen shows them as returned instead of adding tax. Cases whose expected values depend on that basis were reset to Untested: TC-066, TC-068, TC-109.

**Run 2026-10-29 (price basis re-run):** 3 reset case(s) re-executed against the real dev API (`https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`) on branch fix/tax-included-price-masters, HQ account `test_hq_1`, 1440×900 (+1024×768 for layout): 3 OK, read-only (dialog cancelled, no write). Every amount is shown exactly as the API returns it (tax-included), with no tax added.

**2026-11-19 (ladyroom):** TC-125–TC-137 added (Untested) — 13 `[Ladyroom]` cases: E-02 FR-016 / G-01 FR-M002 gating of the bulk-change target plans by every selected member's 性別 (deltas LR1–LR3; gaps AREA-BE / AREA-MOCK / AREA-LIST-GENDER / AREA-BULK). Run them in the 029 plan §9 order (step 11), after 023 has created the QA 主契約; TC-136 is the only write (go-ahead first, last of the slice).

**Run 2026-11-19 (ladyroom, local BE):** TC-125 – TC-137 executed by Claude (black-box / Playwright) on `http://localhost:3000` (branch `feat/e02-area-master`, Next dev webpack) against the **local fitness-backend-v2 BFF `http://localhost:4000`** (the area API is not deployed on dev — 029 plan §7e AREA-BE), HQ `admin@example.com` (magic-link session), roster 41 members, 1440×900 (+1024×768 for TC-137). Result: **2 OK, 3 NG, 8 Block (AREA-LIST-GENDER)**.

- **AREA-LIST-GENDER confirmed**: the dialog reads only `GET /admin/plans?limit=20&sort=name&order=asc` (fetched when the select opens) and never a member's 性別 (no `GET /admin/members/{id}`, even for a 41-member select-all), so every selection — 女性 / 男性 / その他 / 回答しない / 未設定 / mixed — is offered the same 14 plans. TC-126 – TC-130, TC-133 – TC-135 are `Block (AREA-LIST-GENDER)`, observed options in Notes; raise to BE (`MemberListItemDto.gender`).
- **BUG-A01-14 (FE logic, open)**: `genderCondition: nobody` (`QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-NOBODY`) is offered for every selection although it needs no member 性別 — TC-125, TC-131, TC-132.
- **BUG-A01-15 (FE, open)**: no 「変更できる主契約はありません」 disabled state — with an empty plan list the select still reads 選択してください and opens (TC-132).
- **TC-137 NG (BUG-A01-16) → fixed 2026-11-19 (CSS)**: a 100-character target plan widened the dialog body until キャンセル / 実行 left the 1024px viewport; fixed in `src/app/(private)/members/_components/member-bulk-contract-dialog.tsx` and re-verified at 1024 and 1440.
- **AREA-BULK (TC-136) OK on the local BE**: the server refuses the whole job with `422 E-BPC-206` naming the 男性 member — nothing booked, no jobId.
- **Data changed on the local DB (all restored)**: local-seed `JF-9900003` `gender` set to NULL for TC-130 (restored to `male`); an `auth.contracts` projection row (`01a1f239-f424-…`, active) inserted for throw-away `JF-0000002` so the bulk call reached the gender check (the throw-away had none → `E-BPC-203`), deleted afterwards. **Left**: nothing new — no booking, no new member or plan; the QA 主契約 (MC-0010 – MC-0013, MC-0018 – MC-0020) and throw-aways `JF-0000001` / `JF-0000002` stay for the other `[Ladyroom]` units (cleanup per 029 plan §8).

**2026-11-19 — decision (user):** the bulk dialog keeps its current behaviour (no 性別 gating; BE will not add `gender` to the list); the server refusal is the gate. TC-125 – TC-135 rewritten and reset to Untested; BUG-A01-14 / BUG-A01-15 withdrawn; AREA-LIST-GENDER closed; TC-136 re-judged (OK kept).

**Re-run 2026-11-19 after the decision (ladyroom, local BE):** TC-125 – TC-135 executed by Claude (black-box / Playwright) on `http://localhost:3000` (branch `feat/e02-area-master`) against the **local fitness-backend-v2 BFF `http://localhost:4000`**, HQ `admin@example.com`, 1440×900, roster 70 members. Result: **11 OK** (0 NG, 0 Block). Every mismatched selection — 男性 / その他 / 回答しない / 未設定 on `-F` / `-M` / `-SEX-F`, anyone on `-NOBODY`, a member ticked on another page, and a 26-member select-all — is refused as a whole with `422 E-BPC-206`; the toast names the member number(s) (more than three → 「{first three} ほか{n}名」) and asks to deselect, the selection is kept and no job is created. A matching selection books normally (`202`, toast 「1名の主契約変更を予約しました / 2026年12月1日に適用されます」, selection cleared).

- **Throw-away members created (local DB, kept)** through `POST /admin/applications` + `…/approve` in S-001 (JOYFIT24 渋谷 (dev), 主契約 Joyfit24 Standard (dev)): TF `JF-0000006` QA-LR-TF (女性), TO `JF-0000007` (その他), TP `JF-0000008` (回答しない), TU `JF-0000009` (created 男性, `gender` cleared to NULL in the local DB — left NULL), and `JF-0000010` – `JF-0000031` QA-LR-B01 – B22 (odd = 男性, even = 女性) for TC-133 / TC-134. TM = LR-TM `JF-0000002`. To create them, campaign 会員紹介 (JOYFIT24 dev) (`…07f1`) was linked to S-001 through `POST /admin/stores/{id}/campaigns/{campaignId}` and unlinked again right after (S-001's campaign set is as before).
- **Local contract rows added (kept)**: approval leaves no `auth.contracts` projection, so the bulk call stopped at `E-BPC-203`; 27 active `auth.contracts` rows mirroring each throw-away's `contracts.contracts` main contract were inserted for TF, TO, TP, TU, B01 – B22 and TM.
- **Bookings left on the local DB**: job `ad9f1080-c841-4fd0-a4ab-a92c2925f8b3` — TF `JF-0000006` → `QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-F`, 2026-12-01 (TC-132); job `796369c3-bae7-4e90-9a7c-f0da541946ae` — TM `JF-0000002` → `QA-E02-AREA-PLAN-M`, 2026-12-01 (TC-127). Both pending; they give `-F` / `-M` a holder at the effective date. Every other execute was refused and booked nothing.
- LR-TF `JF-0000001` was not used: it already holds an individual `-F` change for 2026-12 (`E-BPC-205` for any month), and the dialog has no month picker (TC-110).

**Re-run 2026-09-21 (second pass)** — the header store scope was switched to the **real store master**, closing gap B3. Six cases that had never been executable ran for the first time: TC-031, TC-046, TC-076, TC-077, TC-078, TC-080 (all OK), and TC-088's previously unverifiable half is now confirmed (scope 「JOYFIT24 渋谷 (dev)」 → 169 rows narrow to 17). The same pass re-confirmed the BE bug and raised one **API-owned question** on the banner's baseline count (TC-097, now `Block` — see the blocked list).

**Added 2026-10-08** — TC-121 (Manager whose home store is not a managed store): `OK`. The reported 「マネージャーで会員一覧が表示されない」 was a 400 caused by the screen sending the home store as `storeId`; fixed by reading `managedStoreIds` from the staff record. See test-plan gap B9.

**Added + run 2026-11-09** — TC-122 – TC-124 (契約種別 filter, delta D14: A-01 FR-001 lists 契約種別, the prototype replaced it with 主契約プラン on 5/24; user decided to offer both). Run on `http://localhost:3000` against the real dev backend `https://api.dev.fitness-v2.okamoto-hd.com`, HQ `test_hq_1`, 1440×900: **3 OK**, no console errors, nothing written to the backend. The by-priority NG column was corrected to 0 (it still carried a closed bug).

**Re-run 2026-09-29** — TC-095 re-executed after BE fixed the `storeId` scope check: `NG` → `OK`, `BUG-A01-12` closed.

**Blocked (1)** — not a screen defect:

- ~~**AREA-LIST-GENDER (8)**: TC-126, TC-127, TC-128, TC-129, TC-130, TC-133, TC-134, TC-135 — `GET /admin/members` carries no `gender`, so the bulk dialog cannot narrow the target plans by the selected members' 性別 (test-plan §6). Ask BE to add `gender` to `MemberListItemDto`; re-run then.~~ → **closed 2026-11-19 by decision**; the 8 cases were rewritten and re-run the same day (all OK).

- ~~**Known gap B3 — the header store-scope switcher's mock ids**~~ → **closed 2026-09-21**: the scope now sends a real store id (`00000000-…-0203`), the roster loads for Manager/Staff/Observer and the six cases it blocked have been executed.
- **No dev account with 2+ accessible stores (1)**: TC-120 — the staff model allows only one direct store or one FC company, and only one dev store belongs to an FC company at all, so no account can hold 2+ stores today. BE must attach a second store to an FC company (investigated 2026-09-21).
- ~~**Known gap B4 — accepted limitation**: TC-111~~ → **fixed and closed 2026-09-22**: the screen now clears the selection on success only, so after a refusal the named members stay selected and can be deselected.
- ~~**Known gap B5 — no injectable data**: TC-082~~ → **closed 2026-09-22**: run at 1024x768 with the value injected into the API response by a Playwright route. The system's real cap is 50 characters per name part (form and `PATCH /admin/members/{memberId}` alike), so 101 characters is the maximum a row can carry — the 255-character assumption in the case was unreachable by design.
- ~~**Data limitation**: TC-051~~ → **closed 2026-09-21** once the roster reached 201 members (9 pages); TC-044 was closed the same day by scoping the header to a store with 0 members.

**Closed 2026-09-22 without new data**: TC-091 (the hyphenated code is handed to the screen through the entry URL, the real API answers 400 and the screen shows its load-failure state) and TC-097 (the `totalAllItems: null` degradation was forced through a route). Two BE items stay open from them: the promotion-code documentation, and whether `totalAllItems` keeps a header-sent `storeId`.

**Asks for BE, raised by this run** (not screen defects): seed a 退会処理待ち and a 強制退会済み member (TC-102), a 休会中 + gate-stopped member (TC-103), a second store on an FC company (TC-120); fix the promotion-code documentation (TC-091); clear the three bookings listed above; ~~fix BUG-A01-12 (an explicit `storeId` overrides the caller's own scope)~~ (done 2026-09-29); and settle whether `totalAllItems` keeps a header-sent `storeId` (TC-097). Also observed: `query=JF` returns **0** members although member numbers start with `JF-`, while the full `JF-9000024` matches — server-side matching semantics, which FR-002/delta D8 leave to the backend, so it is reported rather than failed.

**Deviations recorded as OK-with-note** (for PO): default page size **25** and no 200 option (TC-048, per qa-155 §7); loading shows **20** skeleton rows, not 7 (TC-003); plan and promo option lists are data-driven supersets of the mockup's fixed lists (TC-020, TC-024); brand options order JOYFIT before FIT365 (TC-021); the entry URL uses `last_visit=over_1m` rather than the prototype's `lastVisit=1m-plus` (TC-028); page changes do not scroll because the shell is fixed-height with an inner scroll container (TC-052); the bulk dialog has no month picker although FR-016 allows up to twelve months ahead (TC-110, delta D2); a truncated long name exposes no tooltip (TC-081).

**Withdrawn (4)** — `TC-119` (入会取り消し, withdrawn 2026-09-22: the prototype draws no state for it and the API enum has no 取消 value, so this screen has nothing to assert — the requirement stays with BA/BE, see delta D12); and unchanged from 2026-09-16: `TC-087` / `BUG-A01-06` (full-width digits), `TC-037` / `BUG-A01-02` (null ordering), and the retired `BUG-A01-04` expectation.
